Proof of the Theorem
1.1

We are now prepared to conclude the proof of Theorem
1.1
. For simplicity, we will exclusively derive the energy estimates (
1.34
) and omit details of non-negativity proof of
F^{\varepsilon}
as it closely resembles the proof provided in
[
63
]
.

For this purpose, combing Propositions
2.2
and
2.1
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{i}\Big[\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}^{i}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}+\varepsilon^{1+\kappa}Y\|{\langle v\rangle}w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{i+\kappa}\Big[\|\nabla_{x}^{i}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\Big(\varepsilon^{1+\kappa}+\varepsilon(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big)\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}\Big)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{3+\kappa}\|wh^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{\bf D}}+\sum_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{i}\Big((1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big)\Big[\|\nabla_{x}^{i}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}}

\displaystyle+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\left(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\Big(\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}}+\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{i}_{\bf D}}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\varepsilon^{i}\Big(\|\nabla_{x}^{i}f^{\varepsilon}\|+\varepsilon^{1+\kappa}\|w_{i}\nabla_{x}^{i}h^{\varepsilon}\|\Big),

where
\mathcal{E}(t)
is defined in (
1.27
).

Next, choosing
\varepsilon_{0}\geq\varepsilon>0
sufficiently small such that

C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\left(\frac{-\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{2+\kappa},\qquad\kappa=\frac{1}{3},

we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{E}(t)+\mathcal{D}(t)\lesssim\Big[\varepsilon^{\kappa}+(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\Big]\mathcal{E}(t)+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2},

where
\mathcal{D}(t)
is given by (
1.28
). Then for
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
, we apply Gronwall’s inequality over
t\in[0,\varepsilon^{-\kappa}]
with
\kappa=\frac{1}{3}
to obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{D}(s)\,ds\lesssim\mathcal{E}(0)+1.

This completes the proof of Theorem
1.1
.

3.
Global Hilbert expansion for the non-cutoff VMB system

To justify the validity of the Hilbert expansion (
1.4
) for the non-cutoff VMB system, as discussed in Section
2
,
our first task is to determine the coefficients
[F_{n},E_{n},B_{n}](1\leq n\leq 2k-1)
, which is given by the iteration equations (
1.1.1
). Subsequently, we need to prove the wellposedness of the remainders
[f^{\varepsilon},E^{\varepsilon}_{R},B^{\varepsilon}_{R}]
and
h^{\varepsilon}
via the equations (
1.16
) and (
1.2
) with prescribed initial data (
•
‣
2
) and (
2.2
), respectively.

3.1.
Preliminaries

In this subsection, we gather various estimates related to the collision operators of the non-cutoff VMB system. To facilitate later analysis, we will derive estimates for the linearized collision operators
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}
,
\mathcal{L}_{d}
, and the nonlinear collision operator
\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}
. These estimates are adaptations of those corresponding to the global Maxwellian
\mu
, as established in prior works like
[
1
,
26
]
. To achieve this, we must overcome difficulties arising from the local Maxwellian, such as the non-commutative property of the collision operators
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}
,
\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}
, and the differential operators
\partial^{\alpha}
. Simultaneously, we aim to improve the exponentially weighted estimate for
\Gamma
, previously proven in
[
12
,
21
]
. The improved estimate for
\Gamma
holds significance not only for the weighted estimates associated with the coefficient
F_{1}
but also of its own interest.

3.1.1.
The linearized collision operators

In contrast to the VML case, the expression of
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}
is now less complicated. Similar to Subsection
2.1.1
, we can also establish corresponding estimates on the operators
\mathcal{L}
and
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}
in the non-cutoff VMB case.

Lemma 3.1
.

The linearized collision operators
\mathcal{L}
and
\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}
satisfy the following coercivity estimates:

(i)

For the operator
\mathcal{L}
corresponding to the global Maxwellian
\mu
, it holds that

\big\langle\mathcal{L}h,h\big\rangle\gtrsim|({\bf I}-{\bf P})h|_{\bf D}^{2}.

(3.1)

Moreover, let
|\beta|>0
,
\ell\geq 0
and
q\in[0,\infty)
, for
\eta>0
small enough, there exists
C_{\eta}>0
such that

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\mathcal{L}h,\langle v\rangle^{2\ell}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{\beta}h\right\rangle\geq

\displaystyle\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{\beta}f\right|^{2}_{\bf D}-\eta\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|<|\beta|}\left|w_{\ell}\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}({\bf I}-{\bf P})h\right|_{\bf D}^{2}

(3.2)

\displaystyle-C_{\eta}\left|\chi_{\{|v|\leq 2C_{\eta}\}}h\right|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

(ii)

Let
\mathbf{M}=\mathbf{M}_{[\rho,u,T]},
where
[\rho,u,T]
is determined by Proposition
1.1
, then there exists a constant
\delta>0
such that

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}f,f\big)\geq\delta\big|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})f\big|_{\bf D}^{2}.

(3.3)

Moreover, for
\ell\geq 0
,
q\in[0,\infty)
,
|\beta|>0
, and some small constant
\eta>0
, it holds that

\displaystyle\big(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}f,\langle v\rangle^{2\ell}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}f\big)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\delta\big|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\big|_{\bf D}^{2}-C(\eta+\epsilon_{1})\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|}\big|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}f\big|_{\bf D}^{2}

(3.4)

\displaystyle-C(\eta)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|<|\beta|}\big|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}f\big|^{2}_{L^{2}(B_{C(\eta)})},

and for
|\beta|=0
, it holds that

\displaystyle\big(\partial^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}f,\langle v\rangle^{2\ell}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha}f\big)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\delta\big|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha}f\big|_{\bf D}^{2}-C\epsilon_{1}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}<\alpha}{\bf 1}_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\big|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}f\big|_{\bf D}^{2}

(3.5)

\displaystyle-C(\eta)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\big|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}f\big|^{2}_{L^{2}(B_{C(\eta)})}.

Furthermore, for
|\alpha|>0
, there exists a polynomial
\mathcal{U}(\cdot)
with
\mathcal{U}(0)=0
such that

\displaystyle\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}f,\partial^{\alpha}f\big\rangle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{3\delta}{4}\|\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})f\|_{\bf D}^{2}-C\mathcal{U}(\|\nabla_{x}[\rho,u,T]\|_{W^{|\alpha|-1,\infty}})

(3.6)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})f\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{\bf D}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|f\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}\Big).

Proof.

(
3.1
) is derived from
[
26
, Theorem 8.1, p. 829]
and
[
1
, Theorem 1.1, p. 920]
. Equation (
3.2
) is a direct consequence of Lemmas 2.6 and 2.7 in
[
12
]
. (
3.3
) and (
(ii)
) can be established following the same method as for (
3.1
) and (
3.2
), respectively. It’s important to note that, for any vector indices
\alpha
and
\beta
, the inequality

\displaystyle|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathbf{M}|\lesssim\mu^{5/6}

is valid due to (
1.26
) and the smallness of
\epsilon_{1}
.

In contrast, (
(ii)
) and (
3.6
) are consequences of (
1.26
), (
1.13
), and (
(ii)
).
∎

3.1.2.
Nonlinear collision operators

Recalling that the operators
\Gamma
and
\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}
are defined as

\displaystyle\Gamma(f,g)=\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}(\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}f,\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}g),\quad\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f,g)=\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{C}(\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}f,\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}g).

For later use, we will derive trilinear estimates for the two nonlinear collision operators. The first lemma concerns trilinear estimates of
\Gamma
and
\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}
without velocity weight.

Lemma 3.2
.

Assume
\max\big\{-3,-\frac{3}{2}-2s\big\}<\gamma<0
. It holds that

\displaystyle\big|\big(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(f,g),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big)\big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha,\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}\leq\beta}\Big(\big|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\big|_{L^{2}}\big|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\big|_{\bf D}+\big|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\big|_{\bf D}\big|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\big|_{L^{2}}\Big)\big|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big|_{\bf D}

(3.7)

and

\displaystyle\big|\big(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f,g),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha,\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}\leq\beta}\Big(\big|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\big|_{L^{2}}\big|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\big|_{\bf D}+\big|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\big|_{\bf D}\big|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\big|_{L^{2}}\Big)\big|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big|_{\bf D}

(3.8)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}=\alpha,\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}\leq\beta}{\bf 1}_{|\alpha_{3}|\geq 1}\mathcal{U}(\|\nabla_{x}[\rho,u,T]\|_{W^{|\alpha_{3}|-1,\infty}})

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\big|_{L^{2}}\big|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\big|_{\bf D}+\big|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\big|_{\bf D}\big|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\big|_{L^{2}}\Big)\big|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big|_{\bf D}.

Proof.

(
3.7
) follows from
[
1
, Theorem 1.2, p. 921]
and
[
12
, Proposition 4.4, p. 202]
. with slight modifications. Since the local Maxwellian doesn’t pose significant difficulties, (
3.2
) can be proven similarly.
∎

We now prove exponentially weighted estimates for the nonlinear collision operator
\Gamma
in the following lemma. These improved estimates address the undesirable terms that arise in previous results
[
12
, Lemma 2.3, p. 176]
for
0<s<1
and
[
21
, Lemma 2.4, p. 121]
for
0<s<\frac{1}{2}
, such as
\left|\overline{w}g_{1}\right|_{1+\gamma/2}\big|\mu^{\frac{\lambda_{0}}{128}}g_{2}\big|_{L^{2}}\left|\overline{w}g_{3}\right|_{1+\gamma/2}
. This adjustment allows us to avoid the unmanageable term (
1.41
) when dealing with the following inner product

\big|\big\langle[\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{1},h^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{1})],\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

Our proof relies on a precise angular integration estimate. The estimates derived below are of their own interests and can be applied in further study of the non-cutoff Boltzmann-type equations.

Lemma 3.3
.

Assume
\max\{-3,-\frac{3}{2}-2s\}<\gamma<-2s
for all

0<s<1
, and
\lambda_{0}>0
.

(i)

It holds that

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,g),\overline{w}^{2}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h\right)\right|

(3.9)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum\left\{\left|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{{\bf D}}+\left|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{\bf D}\right\}\left|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\right|_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+\min\left\{\left|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}}\left|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}},\left|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{v}}\left|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\right\}\left|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\right|_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+\sum\left|e^{\frac{\langle v\rangle}{8RT_{c}\ln(e+t)}}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}}\left|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\right|_{\bf D},

where the summation
\sum
is taken over
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha
and
\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}\leq\beta
.

(ii)

For
\ell\geq 0
and
q\in[0,\infty)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Gamma(f,g),\langle v\rangle^{2\ell}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h\right)\right|

(3.10)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum\left\{\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{\bf D}+\left|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{\bf D}\right\}\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\right|_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+\min\left\{\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}}\left|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}},\left|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}}\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\right\}\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\right|_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+\sum\left|e^{{q}\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g\right|_{L^{2}}\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\right|_{\bf D},

where the summation
\sum
is taken over
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}\leq\alpha
and
\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}\leq\beta
.

Proof.

We only prove (
3.9
) since (
3.10
) can be proved in the same way. Note that
[
12
, Lemma 2.3, p. 176]
is a direct conclusion of
[
12
, Lemma 2.1, p. 169]
. We mainly focus on refining the estimate on
I_{3}
in
[
12
, Lemma 2.1]
and omit the estimation of the term
I_{2,2}
, which can be treated in the same way. Other parts of the proof there can remain unchanged.

Noting that

\displaystyle v^{\prime}-v=\left(\frac{v-v_{\ast}}{|v-v_{\ast}|}\cdot\sigma-1\right)\frac{v-v_{\ast}}{2}=\left(\cos\theta-1\right)\frac{v-v_{\ast}}{2},

we have

\displaystyle\overline{w}(v^{\prime})-\overline{w}(v)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(v-v^{\prime})\cdot\nabla_{v}\overline{w}(v)+\frac{1}{2}(v^{\prime}-v)\otimes(v^{\prime}-v):\nabla^{2}_{v}\overline{w}|_{v=v(\tau)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\cos\theta-1\right)\frac{v-v_{\ast}}{2}\nabla_{v}\overline{w}(v)+\frac{1}{2}(v^{\prime}-v)\otimes(v^{\prime}-v):\nabla^{2}_{v}\overline{w}|_{v=v(\tau)}.

This implies

\overline{w}(v^{\prime})-\overline{w}(v)\lesssim\left(\left|\cos\theta-1\right||v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right)\overline{w}(v)\overline{w}(v_{\ast})\langle v_{\ast}\rangle^{2}.

On the other hand, it is straightforward to compute that

\displaystyle|\overline{w}(v^{\prime})-\overline{w}(v)|\lesssim\langle v_{\ast}\rangle^{\ell}\langle v\rangle^{\ell}\exp{\frac{(\langle v_{\ast}\rangle+\langle v\rangle)}{8RT_{c}\ln(e+t)}}\lesssim\overline{w}(v)\overline{w}(v_{\ast}).

Then we can deduce that

\displaystyle\overline{w}(v^{\prime})-\overline{w}(v)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\{\left(\left|\cos\theta-1\right||v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right),1\}\overline{w}(v)\overline{w}(v_{\ast})\langle v_{\ast}\rangle^{2}.

Now we re-estimate the term
I_{3}
in the proof of Lemma 2.1 in
[
12
]
.

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}{\bf B}(v-v_{\ast},\sigma)\mu(v,v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)(\overline{w}(v^{\prime})-\overline{w}(v))dvdv_{\ast}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}{\bf B}(v-u,\sigma)\mu(v,v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)

\displaystyle\times\min\{\left(\left|\cos\theta-1\right||v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right),1\}\overline{w}(v)\overline{w}(v_{\ast})\langle u\rangle^{2}dvdv_{\ast}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}{\bf B}(v-v_{\ast},\sigma)(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\bar{\lambda}}(v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)

\displaystyle\times\min\{\left(\left|\cos\theta-1\right||v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right),1\}\overline{w}(v)\overline{w}(v_{\ast})\langle v_{\ast}\rangle^{2}dvdv_{\ast}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}{\bf B}(v-v_{\ast},\sigma)(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{16}}(v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)

\displaystyle\times\min\{\left(\left|\cos\theta-1\right||v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right),1\}\overline{w}(v)dvdv_{\ast}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}\int^{\frac{\pi}{2}}_{0}\langle v-v_{\ast}\rangle^{\gamma}{\bf b}(\cos\theta)(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{16}}(v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)

\displaystyle\times\min\{\left(\left|\cos\theta-1\right||v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right),1\}\overline{w}(v)\sin\theta dvdv_{\ast}d\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}\langle v-v_{\ast}\rangle^{\gamma}(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{16}}(v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)\overline{w}(v)

\displaystyle\times\int^{\min\{\frac{\pi}{2},|v-v_{\ast}|^{-1}\}}_{0}{\bf b}(\cos\theta)\min\{\left(\left|\cos\theta-1\right||v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right),1\}\sin\theta d\theta dvdv_{\ast}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}\langle v-v_{\ast}\rangle^{\gamma}(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{16}}(v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)\overline{w}(v)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\min\{\frac{\pi}{2},|v-v_{\ast}|^{-1}\}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}{\bf b}(\cos\theta)\min\{\left(\left|\cos\theta-1\right||v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right),1\}\sin\theta d\theta dvdv_{\ast}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}\langle v-v_{\ast}\rangle^{\gamma}(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{16}}(v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)\overline{w}(v)

\displaystyle\times\int^{\min\{\frac{\pi}{2},|v-v_{\ast}|^{-1}\}}_{0}{\bf b}(\cos\theta)\left(\left|\cos\theta-1\right||v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right)\sin\theta d\theta dvdv_{\ast}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}\langle v-v_{\ast}\rangle^{\gamma}(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{16}}(v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)\overline{w}(v)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\min\{\frac{\pi}{2},|v-v_{\ast}|^{-1}\}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}{\bf b}(\cos\theta)\sin\theta d\theta dvdv_{\ast}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}\langle v-v_{\ast}\rangle^{\gamma}(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{16}}(v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)\overline{w}(v)

\displaystyle\times\int^{\min\{\frac{\pi}{2},|v-v_{\ast}|^{-1}\}}_{0}\theta^{-1-2s}\left(\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|+\theta^{2}|v-v_{\ast}|^{2}\right)d\theta dvdv_{\ast}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}}\langle v-v_{\ast}\rangle^{\gamma}(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{16}}(v_{\ast})f(v)g(v_{\ast})h(v)\overline{w}(v)

\displaystyle\times\int_{\min\{\frac{\pi}{2},|v-v_{\ast}|^{-1}\}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\theta^{-1-2s}d\theta dvdv_{\ast}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{32}}g\right|\left|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\overline{w}f\right|\left|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma+2s}{2}}\overline{w}h\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\mu^{\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{32}}g\right|\left|\overline{w}f\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}}\left|\overline{w}h\right|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\gamma}{2}+s}},

where we used the fact that there exist positive constants

\tilde{\lambda},k,\bar{\lambda}
such that

\mu(v,v_{\ast})=(\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v)-\mu^{\tilde{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}))^{k}\mu^{\bar{\lambda}}(v_{\ast}).

∎

3.1.3.
The difference operator
\mathcal{L}_{d}

Our last lemma in this subsection is concerned with some estimates related to the difference operator
\mathcal{L}_{d}
. The proof is omitted as it closely resembles that of Lemma
2.6
for the VML case.

Lemma 3.4
.

Let
\rho(t,x),u(t,x)
and
T(t,x)
satisfy (
1.13
), and suppose (
1.26
) is valid. For
\overline{w}(t,v)=\exp\big(\frac{\langle v\rangle}{8RT_{c}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}\big)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big\rangle\big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\big\|\overline{w}h\big\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{\bf D}}^{2},

(3.11)

and for
\ell\geq 0
,
q\in[0,\infty)
,

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}_{d}[g],{\langle v\rangle}^{2\ell}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big\rangle\big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}h\big\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{\bf D}}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\ell}e^{q\langle v\rangle}h\big\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{\bf D}}.

(3.12)

Moreover, if we set
\sqrt{\mathbf{M}}f=\sqrt{\mu}h
, then it holds that

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big\rangle\big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\Big(\big\|({\bf I}-{\bf P})h\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{\bf D}}^{2}+\big\|f\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{\bf D}}^{2}\Big)

and

\displaystyle\big(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}[h],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big)\geq

\displaystyle\delta\big|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h\big|_{\bf D}^{2}-\eta\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|}\big|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta^{\prime}}h\big|_{\bf D}^{2}-C(\eta)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}f|^{2}_{L^{2}},

(3.13)

where
\eta>0
is a small constant and
C(\eta)
is some large constant depending on
\eta
.

3.2.
Estimates of
f^{\varepsilon},E_{R}^{\varepsilon},B_{R}^{\varepsilon}

In this subsection, we aim to derive estimates for the remainders
f^{\varepsilon}
,
E_{R}^{\varepsilon}
, and
B_{R}^{\varepsilon}
, which satisfy the equations (
1.16
) and (
1.17
) with the initial datum (
•
‣
2
) for the VMB case.

As in Section
2
for the VML case, we focus solely on the
a priori
energy estimate (
1.34
), made under the
a priori
assumption with the same form as (
2.26
).
The following two lemmas deal with the terms related to the Lorentz force, and their proofs closely resemble those in Lemma
2.7
and Lemma
2.8
. The primary difference is that, unlike the VML case, we can now bound the terms involving
\nabla_{v}f
using the energy functional
\mathcal{E}(t)
of
h^{\varepsilon}
when the velocity
v
is large. As such, we provide the estimates and omit the proof details for brevity.

Lemma 3.5
.

Assume that
f^{\varepsilon},E_{R}^{\varepsilon},B_{R}^{\varepsilon}
and
h^{\varepsilon}
is the smooth solution to the Cauchy problem (
1.16
), (
1.17
), (
1.2
), (
2.25
), and (
2.26
) corresponding to the VMB case holds. Then for
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big(f^{\varepsilon}\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\big)\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big),4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}Z_{3,\alpha,\beta}(t),

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],2\pi T\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\mathbf{Z}_{3,\alpha,\beta}(t),

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{(v-u)}{T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],2\pi T\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\varepsilon\mathbf{Z}_{3,\alpha,\beta}(t),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\Big)\cdot\frac{(v-u)}{T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],2\pi T\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{1-\kappa}\mathbf{Z}_{3,\alpha,\beta}(t),

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{Z}_{3,\alpha,\beta}(t):=

\displaystyle\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{v}}+\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big).

Lemma 3.6
.

Under the same assumptions in Lemma
3.5
, for
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4
and
t\in[0,\varepsilon^{-\kappa}]
with
\kappa=\frac{1}{3}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}{\bf 1}_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq 1}Z_{4,\alpha,\beta}(t),

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\varepsilon{\bf 1}_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq 1}\mathbf{Z}_{4,\alpha,\beta}(t),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}+\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\frac{\nabla_{v}F_{n}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}}}\Big],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{2\kappa}\Big[\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{v}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}+{\bf 1}_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq 1}\mathbf{Z}_{4,\alpha,\beta}(t)\Big],

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{Z}_{4,\alpha,\beta}(t)

\displaystyle:=\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{v}}+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|+1,\atop{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|+1,\atop{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big).

Now, we are prepared to derive energy estimates for
f^{\varepsilon}
,
E_{R}^{\varepsilon}
, and
B_{R}^{\varepsilon}
. Let’s begin with the energy estimate that does not involve velocity derivatives.

Proposition 3.1
.

Under the same assumptions in Lemma
3.5
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 4}\Big[\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}\Big(\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\delta\varepsilon^{|\alpha|-1}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 4}\|\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{|\alpha|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big]\Big[\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}

(3.14)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|-1,\atop{|\beta^{\prime}|=1,|\alpha|\geq 1}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\|_{\bf D}^{2}+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\Big)\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 4}{\bf 1}_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\varepsilon^{|\alpha|-2}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})f\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{\bf D}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\sum\limits_{|\alpha|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{k+|\alpha|}(1+t)^{2k}\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|.

Proof.

The proof is divided into two steps.

Step 1. Basic energy estimate of the remainders.
In this step, we derive the
L^{2}
estimate on
[f^{\varepsilon},E_{R}^{\varepsilon},B_{R}^{\varepsilon}]
. Corresponding to (
2.39
), in the VMB case, we can apply (
3.3
) to have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

(3.15)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{2}\big|\big\langle\partial_{t}Tf^{\varepsilon},4\pi Rf^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\Big|\Big\langle\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot u\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}},4\pi f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot(v-u)f^{\varepsilon},2\pi f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle f^{\varepsilon}\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\big)\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}},4\pi RTf^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),4\pi RTf^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}\big|\big\langle[\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},f^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})\big],4\pi RTf^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot(v-u)f^{\varepsilon},2\pi f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{v}F_{n},4\pi RTf^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\Big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\Big)\cdot(v-u)f^{\varepsilon},2\pi f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\mathcal{Q}_{0},4\pi RTf^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

Now we estimate the terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.15
) individually.

In view of (
1.13
), the upper bound of the first two terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.15
) is

(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\epsilon_{1}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big).
For the 3rd, 4th, 7th-9th terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.15
),
we use Lemma
3.5
and Lemma
3.6
with
\beta=0
to bound them by

C\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{2\kappa}\Big]\Big[\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Z_{4,0,0}(t)\Big].

Noting that corresponding to (
2.37
) and (
2.59
),

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}}\Big)\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}},\qquad\varepsilon^{k-1}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}}\lesssim 1,\qquad k\geq 4,

(3.16)

by the
a priori
assumption (
2.26
) in the VMB case. Then for the 5th and 6th terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.15
),
similar to the estimates (
2.2
) and (
2.2
), we can use (
3.2
) and (
4.3
) to bound them by

o(1)\varepsilon^{-1}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{\kappa}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}.

For the last term on the R.H.S. of (
3.15
), similar to the VML case, we can
use (
4.3
), (
4.4
), and Cauchy’s inequality to have

\varepsilon^{k}\big\langle\mathcal{Q}_{0},4\pi RTf^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\lesssim\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|.

(3.17)

Substituting the above estimates into (
3.15
) leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\Big[(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big]\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\Big)\|h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|.

(3.18)

Step 2. Estimates on the pure spatial derivatives of the remainders.

In this step, we proceed to derive estimates for
\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}
,
\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}
, and
\partial^{\alpha}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}
for
1\leq|\alpha|\leq N
.

Proceeding similar derivation as that in (
2.44
), one gets

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|[\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}[f^{\varepsilon}],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle

(3.19)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\big|\big\langle\partial_{t}T\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}+\partial^{\alpha}\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{u}{T},4\pi R\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}<\alpha}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}\Big[\frac{v-u}{T}\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big],2\pi T\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\alpha^{\prime}<\alpha}\big|\big\langle v\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\big(\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\left[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],4\pi T\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[f^{\varepsilon}\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\big)\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}\big|\big\langle[\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},f^{\varepsilon})+\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})\big],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{(v-u)}{2T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],4\pi T\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}+\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{v}F_{n}\Big],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\Big)\cdot\frac{(v-u)}{2T}f^{\varepsilon}\Big],4\pi T\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\mathcal{Q}_{0},4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

For the 2nd term on the L.H.S. of (
3.19
), applying (
1.13
) and (
3.6
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}f^{\varepsilon},4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{3\delta}{4\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{\bf D}^{2}-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{x}L^{2}_{\bf D}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big).

We now turn to estimate terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.19
) separately.

From (
1.13
), we have the upper bound of
the first three terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.19
) as

C\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}\big)
.

It follows from Lemma
3.5
and Lemma
3.6
for
\beta=0
that the 4th-6th, 9th-12th terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.19
) can be dominated by

\displaystyle C\Big[(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\epsilon_{1}+\varepsilon^{2\kappa}\Big]\Big[\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 1,\atop{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\big\|_{\bf D}^{2}+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\Big)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 1,\atop{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big].

For the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.19
), we
use (
1.13
), (
3.2
), (
3.16
) and Sobolev’s inequalities to have

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{k-1}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big(\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{{\bf D}}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{{\bf D}}+\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}<\alpha}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{{\bf D}}\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{{\bf D}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{\bf D}}^{2}+\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon\Big]\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.

Similarly, for the 8th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.19
), we further use (
4.3
) to get

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}\big|\big\langle[\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},f^{\varepsilon})+\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})\big],4\pi RT\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)\big(\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{\bf D}}\big)\Big(\|\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}+\|\Lbrack\partial^{\alpha},{{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}}\Rbrack[f^{\varepsilon}]\|\Big)

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{1-p_{0}}\big(\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{x}L^{2}_{\bf D}}\big)\Big(\|\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}L^{2}_{v}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{1-2\kappa}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}.

Similar to (
3.17
),
we can deduce from (
4.3
) and (
4.4
) that the last term on the R.H.S. of (
3.19
) can be dominated by

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}^{2}+\frac{C\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{x}L^{2}_{\bf D}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\Big[\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|\Big].

Plugging the above estimates into (
3.19
), and multiplying it by
\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 4}\Big[\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}\Big(\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\delta\varepsilon^{|\alpha|-1}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 4}\|\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big]\Big[\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}

(3.20)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 1,\atop{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\Big)\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 4}\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\Big(\varepsilon^{|\alpha|-2}\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})f\|_{H^{|\alpha|-1}_{x}L^{2}_{\bf D}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big),

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2k+2}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\sum\limits_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{k+|\alpha|}(1+t)^{2k}\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|.

Finally, (
3.24
) follows from (
3.2
), (
3.2
), this ends the proof of Proposition
3.1
.
∎

Next we will estimate the mixed space-velocity derivatives of
f^{\varepsilon}
. To do this, we first
apply the micro-projection operator into (
1.16
) to have

\displaystyle\Big[\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}-\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}\Big]({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]

\displaystyle+\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}[f^{\varepsilon}]}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big[\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}-\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}\Big]\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k-1}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\Big[\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},f^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})\Big]

(3.21)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\left(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]-\varepsilon^{k}\left(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big[\left(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]+({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})\Big[\left(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\frac{\nabla_{v}F_{n}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}}}\Big]\Big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big[\left(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\right)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big]+\varepsilon^{k}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})\mathcal{Q}_{0}+\Lbrack{\bf P},\tau_{EB}\Rbrack f^{\varepsilon},

where
\Lbrack{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}},\tau_{EB}\Rbrack
is a commutative operator with the following form:

\displaystyle\Lbrack{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}},\tau_{EB}\Rbrack=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}-\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}+\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big[\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}-\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}\Big]\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{k}\left[\left(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}-\left(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}\right]

\displaystyle-\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\left[\left(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}-\left(E_{n}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{n}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}\right].

Proposition 3.2
.

Under the same assumptions in Lemma
3.5
, for
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
and
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4
with
|\beta|\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\sum\limits_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}+\delta\sum\limits_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|-1}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{|\alpha|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}\Big[(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\epsilon_{1}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big]\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}+\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|+1}C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|+1,\atop{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)

(3.22)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1}\varepsilon^{2k+1+|\alpha|+|\beta|}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

Proof.

By applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
to (
3.2
) and multiplying the result by
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]
, we can make use of (
(ii)
) without the weight function to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}^{2}

(3.23)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|<|\beta|}\big|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\big|_{\bf D}^{2}+\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\big(v\cdot\nabla_{x}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\big),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})f^{\varepsilon}\Big[\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}-\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}\Big]\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k-1}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},f^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma_{\mathbf{M}}(f^{\varepsilon},\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\left(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\left(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\left(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})\Big[\left(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{v}F_{n}\Big]\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\left(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\right)\cdot\frac{v-u}{2RT}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\varepsilon^{k}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})\mathcal{Q}_{0}+\Lbrack{\bf P},\tau_{EB}\Rbrack f^{\varepsilon}\Big],\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\Big\rangle\Big|.

Now we estimate terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.23
).

The 2nd term on the R.H.S. of (
3.23
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle C\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{|\alpha|+1}_{x}H^{|\beta|-1}_{\bf D}}\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{|\gamma+2s|}{2}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{|\alpha|+1}_{x}H^{|\beta|-1}_{\bf D}}^{2}

\displaystyle+o(1)\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\Big(\int_{{\langle v\rangle}^{2|\gamma+2s|}\leq\varepsilon^{-|\gamma+2s|}}+\int_{{\langle v\rangle}^{2|\gamma+2s|}\geq\varepsilon^{-|\gamma+2s|}}\Big)\left|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\right|^{2}\,dvdx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{|\alpha|+1}_{x}H^{|\beta|-1}_{\bf D}}^{2}+\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\Big)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\big\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2},

where we used similar estimates to those in (
2.32
) in the last inequality.

It follows from Lemma
3.5
and Lemma
3.6
that the 3rd-5th, 8th-12th terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.23
) can be dominated by

\displaystyle C\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{2\kappa}\Big]\Big[\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|+1,\atop{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}+C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\Big(-\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\Big)\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)\Big].

For the 6th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.23
), we can use (
2.26
) for the VMB case, (
3.2
), and Sobolev’s inequalities to directly bound it by

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}^{2}+C\varepsilon\Big(\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{\bf D}}^{2}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\Big).

Via (
4.3
), the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.23
) can be controlled similarly by

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big(\|{f^{\varepsilon}}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{\bf D}}\Big).

As in (
3.17
),
the upper bound of the last term on the R.H.S. of (
3.23
) is

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|_{\bf D}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|+1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{v}}\Big).

Finally, plugging the above estimates into (
3.19
), we multiply the result by
\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|}
, sum over
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1
, and arrive at (
3.2
) by the smallness of
\epsilon_{1}
.
∎

Combining Proposition
2.1
and Proposition
3.2
, one can deduce the following total energy estimate of
f^{\varepsilon}
by leveraging the smallness of
\epsilon_{1}
and
\varepsilon
.

Proposition 3.3
.

Assume that the assumptions in Lemma
3.5
hold. Then for
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
with
\kappa=\frac{1}{3}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\sum\limits_{|\alpha|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}\partial^{\alpha}[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}+\sum\limits_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}\Big)

(3.24)

\displaystyle+\delta\sum\limits_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|-1}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big]\mathcal{E}(t)+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}(t)},

where
\mathcal{E}(t)
is defined in Lemma
2.9
.

3.3.
Estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}

In this subsection, we focus on the weighted energy estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}
, which are divided into the spatial derivatives estimates part and the space-velocity derivatives estimates part.

3.3.1.
The spatial derivatives Estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}

Compared with the time-dependent exponential weight function
\exp\left(\frac{\langle v\rangle^{2}}{8RT_{c}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}\right)
in the VML case, the weight function available for the non-cutoff VMB case is
\overline{w}=\exp\left(\frac{\langle v\rangle}{8RT_{c}\ln(\mathrm{e}+t)}\right)
, which results in weaker additional dissipation terms
Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2},|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4
.

Our first lemma concerns terms involving the velocity growth
|v|
in the presence of the electric field. Since the velocity growth in our additional dissipation terms
Y(t)\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}
(
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4
) is also of order 1, we need to bound these terms by the additional dissipation terms only, instead of controlling them through interpolation between the additional dissipation norms and the weighted norms of
h^{\varepsilon}
as in (
2.48
).

Lemma 3.7
.

Assuming the conditions in Lemma
3.5
are satisfied, then for
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4
and
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}Y(t)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\varepsilon Y(t)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{E_{n}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{\kappa^{-}}Y(t)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}.

(3.25)

where
\kappa^{-}
denote any postive number strictly smaller than
\kappa=\frac{1}{3}
.

Proof.

For brevity, let’s verify (
3.25
) since the other two inequalities can be treated similarly. To do this, we use (
1.13
) and (
4.4
) to have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{E_{n}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}(1+t)^{n}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon(1+t)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}\lesssim\,\varepsilon(1+t)^{2}\ln(1+t)Y(t)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\kappa^{-}}Y(t)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}

for
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
with
\kappa=\frac{1}{3}
. Here,
\kappa^{-}
is defined earlier in the text.
∎

The next lemma focuses on controlling the velocity derivative terms caused by the Lorentz force. The most complicated case involves estimating terms such as
\big(v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}
, where both velocity growth and velocity derivatives are present simultaneously. Similar to Lemma
3.7
, we are able to bound these terms using the additional dissipation terms
Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}(|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4)
. These additional dissipation terms are induced by our weight function
\overline{w}
.

Lemma 3.8
.

Assume that the assumptions in Lemma
3.5
hold. Then for
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4
,
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
, one has

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E+v\times B\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\epsilon_{1}{\bf Z}_{5,\alpha,\beta}(t),

(3.26)

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|\lesssim\,\varepsilon{\bf Z}_{5,\alpha,\beta}(t),

(3.27)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\big[\big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{v}F_{n}\big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

(3.28)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}+\varepsilon^{3-2\kappa}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}\Big)+\varepsilon^{\kappa^{-}}{\bf Z}_{5,\alpha,\beta}(t),

where

{\bf Z}_{5,\alpha,\beta}(t):=Y(t)\Big(\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+{\bf I}_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq 1}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|<|\alpha|+|\beta|}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big).

Proof.

For brevity, we will prove (
3.27
) and omit the proof of (
3.26
) and (
3.28
).

From (
3.16
) and Sobolev’s inequalities, we can obtain that for
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
,

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{k}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta-\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\big(\overline{w}^{2}\big)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta\atop{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|>0}}{\bf 1}_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq 1}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta-\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{4}}\Big){\bf Z}_{5,\alpha,\beta}(t)\lesssim\varepsilon{\bf Z}_{5,\alpha,\beta}(t).

Here in the third line of the above estimate, we used the integration by parts w.r.t.
v
when
|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|=0
and the fact
\nabla_{v}\cdot\big(v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)=0
.
∎

With the above preparations in hand,
now we turn to the weighted energy estimates on
h^{\varepsilon}
. The following proposition is about the estimate of the pure spatial derivatives of
h^{\varepsilon}
.

Proposition 3.4
.

Assume that the assumptions in Lemma
3.5
hold. For
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+1+\kappa}\Big(\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}\Big)

(3.29)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\;\varepsilon^{\kappa}\mathcal{E}(t)+\left(\epsilon_{1}+\varepsilon^{\kappa^{-}}\right)\mathcal{D}(t)+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2},

where
\mathcal{E}(t)
is defined in (
1.4.2
), and
\mathcal{D}(t)
is given in (
1.4.2
).

Proof.

The proof is divided into two steps.

Step 1: Basic Weighted Energy Estimate of
h^{\varepsilon}
.
In this step, we aim to establish the following weighted
L^{2}
estimate for
h^{\varepsilon}
:

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big(\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big)+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

(3.30)

To prove this, we first take the
L^{2}
inner product of
\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}
with (
1.2
) and use (
3.13
) with
|\beta|=0
to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}

(3.31)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\big\langle\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}],\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\Big|\Big\langle\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{RT}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M},\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\Big|\Big\langle\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}\big|\big\langle[\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},h^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})],\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{\nabla_{v}F_{n}}{\sqrt{\mu}},\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\frac{E_{n}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\mathcal{Q}_{1},\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

We can now proceed to estimate each term on the R.H.S. of (
3.31
).
For the 2nd term on the R.H.S. of (
3.31
), we use (
3.11
) to bound it by

C\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon^{-1}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}
.
The upper bound of the 3rd term on the R.H.S. of (
3.31
) is

o(1)\varepsilon^{-1}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}+C\varepsilon\big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\big)
.
From Lemma
3.7
and Lemma
3.8
, we can see that the 4th, 5th, 8th-11th terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.31
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle C\left(\epsilon_{1}+\varepsilon^{\kappa^{-}}\right)Y(t)\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}+C\varepsilon^{3-2\kappa}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Big).

For the 6th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.31
), we can use (
3.9
), (
3.16
), and Sobolev’s inequalities to bound it by

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{k-1}\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-1}\|h^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}\lesssim\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}.

For the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.31
), we can use (
4.3
) to bound it similarly by

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{\bf D}^{2}+C(1+t)^{2}\varepsilon\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|_{\bf D}^{2}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}.

As in (
3.17
), the last term on the R.H.S. of (
3.31
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\mathcal{Q}_{1},\overline{w}^{2}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

Collecting the above estimates in (
3.31
) gives (
3.3.1
).

Step 2. High-order derivative estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}
with weight.

In this step, we continue to deduce the estimate of
\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|
with
1\leq|\alpha|\leq N
.

Next, taking the
L^{2}
inner product of
\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}
with (
2.3
) in the non-cutoff VMB case and applying (
3.13
) with
|\beta|=0
, one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

(3.32)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{RT}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\big[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}\big|\big\langle[\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},h^{\varepsilon})+\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big[\big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{v}F_{n}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Big(\frac{E_{n}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\mathcal{Q}_{1},\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

We can now estimate the R.H.S. of (
3.32
) individually.

For the 2nd term on the R.H.S. of (
3.32
), from (
3.11
), its upper bound is given by

C\epsilon_{1}\varepsilon^{-1}\big(\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}+\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}<\alpha}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}\big).

The 3rd term on the R.H.S. of (
3.32
) can be dominated by

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}+C\varepsilon\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}\Big).

By applying Lemma
3.7
and Lemma
3.8
, we can bound the 4th, 5th, 8th-11th terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.32
) by

\displaystyle C\left(\epsilon_{1}+\varepsilon^{\kappa^{-}}\right)Y(t)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,\atop|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{3-2\kappa}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}\Big).

For the 6th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.32
), we use (
3.9
), (
3.16
), and Sobolev’s inequalities to obtain

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha}{\bf I}_{|\alpha_{1}|\leq 2}\left\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{{\bf D}}\left\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{{\bf D}}

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k-1}\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha}{\bf I}_{|\alpha_{1}|>2}\left\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|\left\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{\bf D}}\left\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{{\bf D}}

(3.33)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\|\overline{w}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{4}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\left\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}.

For the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.32
),
we use (
4.1
) to similarly obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}\big|\big\langle[\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},h^{\varepsilon})+\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}[\varepsilon(1+t)]^{n-1}(1+t)\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\Big(\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{{\bf D}}+\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|\Big)\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{{\bf D}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\Big(\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{{\bf D}}^{2}+\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}\Big),

for
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
with
\kappa=\frac{1}{3}
. By a similar way as that in (
3.17
), one gets

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}\mathcal{Q}_{1},\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

Putting the above estimates into (
3.32
) with
\varepsilon
sufficiently small, we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}

(3.34)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big(\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha}\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}<\alpha}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}+\epsilon_{1}Y(t)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,\atop|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

Multiplying (
3.34
) by
\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+1+\kappa}
, taking summation over
1\leq|\alpha|\leq 4
, and combing with
\varepsilon^{1+\kappa}\times\eqref{L2h1 VMB}
give (
3.29
).
This ends the proof of Proposition
3.4
.
∎

3.3.2.
The space-velocity derivatives Estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}

In this part, we are devoted to the space-velocity derivatives estimates of
h^{\varepsilon}
, with weight. As a preparation, we first estimate the delicate transport term. Thanks to the additional dissipation induced by our weight functions
\overline{w}
and the coercivity of the linearized Boltzmann collision operator, we can control this term via interpolation inequalities.

Lemma 3.9
.

For
|\beta|\geq 1
,
t\leq\epsilon^{-\kappa}
with
\kappa=\frac{1}{3}
, it holds that

\displaystyle|\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\epsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\rangle|

(3.35)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{\bf D}^{2}+Y(t)\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\overline{w}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha|+1}\nabla^{|\beta|-1}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{\bf D}+Y(t)\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha|+1}\nabla^{|\beta|-1}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}\Big).

Proof.

It is straightforward to see that

\displaystyle|\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\epsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\rangle|\lesssim\big\|\overline{w}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha|+1}\nabla^{|\beta|-1}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|\left\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\right\|.

We further bound this term via interpolation inequalities as follows:
