\displaystyle|\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\epsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\rangle|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\big\|\overline{w}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha|+1}\nabla^{|\beta|-1}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{\bf D}\Big)^{\frac{1}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}\Big(\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha|+1}\nabla^{|\beta|-1}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|\Big)^{\frac{|\gamma+2s|}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}

\displaystyle\times\Big(\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{\bf D}\Big)^{\frac{1}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}\Big(\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|\Big)^{\frac{|\gamma+2s|}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}Y(t)^{\frac{-|\gamma+2s|}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{\bf D}^{2}+Y(t)\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\overline{w}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha|+1}\nabla^{|\beta|-1}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{\bf D}+Y(t)\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha|+1}\nabla^{|\beta|-1}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}\Big).

Noting that for
t\leq\epsilon^{-\kappa}
with
\kappa=\frac{1}{3}
and
|\gamma+2s|<\frac{3}{2}
,

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}Y(t)^{\frac{-|\gamma+2s|}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}\varepsilon^{\frac{-|\gamma+2s|\kappa^{-}}{1+|\gamma+2s|}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{1+3/2}-\frac{3/2}{3(1+3/2)}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}},

we further obtain (
3.35
).

∎

Now we are ready to give the energy estimates on
\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}
with
|\beta|\geq 1
in the following:

Proposition 3.5
.

Assume that the assumptions in Lemma
3.5
hold. For
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|+1+\kappa}

\displaystyle\Big(\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\kappa}\mathcal{E}(t)+\epsilon_{1}\mathcal{D}(t)+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

(3.36)

Proof.

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1
, applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
to (
1.2
), one has

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle+\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}[v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}]+\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)}{RT}\cdot\big(v-u\big)\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M}\Big]

\displaystyle+\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big]-\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\big[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big]+\frac{\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}[h^{\varepsilon}]}{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}]}{\varepsilon}+\varepsilon^{k-1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon})+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}[\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},h^{\varepsilon})+\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})]

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big]-\varepsilon^{k}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big]

(3.37)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{v}F_{n}\Big]

\displaystyle-\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big(\frac{E_{n}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big)+\varepsilon^{k}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{Q}_{1}.

Next, taking the
L^{2}
inner product of
\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}
with (
3.3.2
) and applying (
3.13
), one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

(3.38)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{v}}+o(1)\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|}\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{\bf D}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+|\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\epsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\rangle|+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}_{d}[h^{\varepsilon}],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\frac{v-u}{RT}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{M}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{E\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\big[\big(E+v\times B\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}\big|\big\langle[\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},h^{\varepsilon})+\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{k}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\Big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|+\varepsilon^{k}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\big)\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\big(E_{R}^{\varepsilon}+v\times B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\big)\cdot\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla_{v}F_{n}\Big],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n}\Big|\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big(\frac{E_{n}\cdot v}{2RT_{c}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\Big|+\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{Q}_{1},\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|.

We now estimate the R.H.S. of (
3.38
) individually.

In view of Lemma
3.9
, the 2nd term on the R.H.S. of (
3.38
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{\bf D}^{2}+Y(t)\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\|\overline{w}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha|+1}\nabla^{|\beta|-1}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{\bf D}+Y(t)\big\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\nabla_{x}^{|\alpha|+1}\nabla^{|\beta|-1}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}\Big).

The upper bound of the 3rd term on the R.H.S. of (
3.38
) is

C\epsilon_{1}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}
by (
3.11
) and
the 4th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.38
) is dominated by

\displaystyle\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}+C\varepsilon\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}\Big).

Applying Lemma
3.7
and Lemma
3.8
, we can bound the 5th, 6th, 9th-12th terms on the R.H.S. of (
3.38
) by

\displaystyle\,C\left(\epsilon_{1}+\varepsilon^{\kappa^{-}}\right)Y(t)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|,\atop|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|+1}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{3-2\kappa}\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}\Big)+\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}.

For the 7th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.38
), similar to (
3.3.1
), we have

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},h^{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}.

For the 8th term on the R.H.S. of (
3.38
),
we get from (
4.3
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{n-1}\big|\big\langle[\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n},h^{\varepsilon})+\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma(h^{\varepsilon},\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n})],\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\sum_{n=1}^{2k-1}[\varepsilon(1+t)]^{n-1}(1+t)\big\|\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\Big(\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{{\bf D}}+\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|\Big)\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{{\bf D}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{{\bf D}}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\leq\beta}\Big(\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{{\bf D}}^{2}+\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}\Big).

As in (
3.17
), for the last term on the R.H.S. of (
3.38
), one has

\displaystyle\big|\big\langle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{Q}_{1},\overline{w}^{2}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\big|\lesssim\frac{o(1)}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

Putting the above estimates into (
3.38
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

(3.39)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{v}}+o(1)\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|}\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{\bf D}^{2}\Big)+\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\varepsilon^{\kappa}\big\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\varepsilon\Big(\|E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}+\|B_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{|\alpha|}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\left(\epsilon_{1}+\varepsilon^{\kappa^{-}}\right)Y(t)\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|+|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta^{\prime}}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

Now multiplying (
3.39
) by
\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|+1+\kappa}
and taking summation over
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1
, one deduces (
3.5
).
∎

Finally, we
collect (
3.29
) and (
3.5
) together to obtain the following total energy estimate for
h^{\varepsilon}
.

Proposition 3.6
.

Assume that the assumptions in Lemma
3.5
hold. For
t\leq\varepsilon^{-\kappa}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|+1+\kappa}\Big(\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\kappa}\mathcal{E}(t)+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

3.4.
Proof of the Theorem
1.2

We are now ready to complete the proof of Theorem
1.2
. For brevity, we will derive the energy estimates (
1.34
) and omit the details of the non-negativity proof of
F^{\varepsilon}
.

For this purpose, combining Propositions
3.6
and
3.3
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Bigg(\sum\limits_{|\alpha|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|}\big(\|\sqrt{4\pi RT}\partial^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}E_{R}^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}B^{\varepsilon}_{R}\|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,|\beta|\geq 1}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|+1+\kappa}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\|^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\delta\sum\limits_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|-1}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I}-{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4}\varepsilon^{|\alpha|+|\beta|+1+\kappa}\Big(Y(t)\big\|{\langle v\rangle}^{\frac{1}{2}}\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}+\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon}\|\overline{w}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}]\|^{2}_{\bf D}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big[\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{\kappa}\Big]\mathcal{E}(t)+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}(t)},

Here,
\mathcal{E}(t)
is defined in (
1.4.2
). By choosing
\varepsilon_{1}\geq\varepsilon>0
sufficiently small such that

C_{\epsilon_{1}}\exp\left(\frac{-\epsilon_{1}}{8C_{0}RT^{2}_{c}\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\leq\varepsilon_{1}^{2+\kappa},\qquad\kappa=\frac{1}{3},

and recalling the definition of
\mathcal{D}(t)
in (
1.4.2
), we get

\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}(t)+\mathcal{D}(t)\lesssim\Big[\varepsilon^{\kappa}+(1+t)^{-p_{0}}+\varepsilon^{k}(1+t)^{2k}\Big]\big[\mathcal{E}(t)+1\big]+\varepsilon^{2k+1}(1+t)^{4k+2}.

This yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{D}(s)\,ds\lesssim\mathcal{E}(0)+1

from Gronwall’s inequality over
t\in[0,\varepsilon^{-\kappa}]
with
\kappa=\frac{1}{3}
. Then, (
1.34
) follows.

4.
Appendix

In this Appendix, we will illustrate the process of determining the coefficients
F_{n},E_{n},B_{n}
for all
1\leq n\leq 2k-1
in the expansion (
1.4
).
The construction of coefficients for the non-cutoff VMB case is presented in detail, while the construction of coefficients for the VML system is omitted for brevity.

4.1.
The coefficients of the VML system

This subsection is concerned with the construction of coefficients
[F_{n},E_{n},B_{n}]
for all
1\leq n\leq 2k-1
in the expansion (
1.4
).
It can be stated as in the following lemma, whose proof is omitted since it is similar to that in Lemma
4.2
.

Lemma 4.1
.

Under the condition (
1.29
), coefficients
[F_{n},E_{n},B_{n}]
for all
1\leq n\leq 2k-1
are well-posed. Moreover, for
N\geq 2
,
q\in(0,1)
, and
\ell\geq 0
, there exists
C_{1}>0
such that

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N+2k-2n+2}\left|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\left(\frac{F_{n}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}}}\right)\right|\leq C_{1}\langle v\rangle^{-\ell+2n}\mathbf{M}^{\frac{q}{2}}(1+t)^{n}

(4.1)

and

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq N+2k-2n+2}|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[E_{n},B_{n}]|\leq C_{1}(1+t)^{n},

(4.2)

where
C_{1}>0
and depends on
N
,
q
, and initial data of
F_{n},E_{n},B_{n}
.

4.2.
The coefficients of the non-cutoff VMB system

Our results for the non-cutoff VMB system are the following

Lemma 4.2
.

Under the condition (
1.33
), coefficients
[F_{n},E_{n},B_{n}]
for all
1\leq n\leq 2k-1
are well-posed. Moreover, for
N\geq 4
,
q\in(0,1)
, and
\ell\geq 0
, there exists
C_{2}>0
such that

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N+4k-2n+2}\left|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\left(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n}\right)\right|\leq C_{2}\langle v\rangle^{-\ell+n(1+|\gamma+2s|)}e^{-q\langle v\rangle}(1+t)^{n}

(4.3)

and

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq N+4k-2n+2}|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[E_{n},B_{n}]|\leq C_{2}(1+t)^{n},

(4.4)

where
C_{2}>0
and depends on
N
,
q
, and initial data of
F_{n},E_{n},B_{n}
.

Proof.

Our proof is divided into two steps.

Step 1. Solvability of the coefficients.
In this step, we will solve the coefficients
F_{n},E_{n}
and
B_{n}
around the local Maxwellian
\mathbf{M}
.
The main argument applied here is originally used in

[
28
]
for the case of cutoff Boltzmann equation and Landau equation around global Maxwellian.
Setting
F_{n}=\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}f_{n}
and recalling the definition (
1.25
), we write

\displaystyle{\bf P}_{\mathbf{M}}[f_{n}]=

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{n}}{\sqrt{\rho}}\chi_{0}+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{3}\frac{1}{\sqrt{R\rho T}}u_{n}^{i}\chi_{i}+\frac{T_{n}}{\sqrt{6\rho}}\chi_{4},

\displaystyle F_{n}=

\displaystyle\Big[\frac{\rho_{n}}{\rho}\mathbf{M}+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{3}\frac{v^{i}-u^{i}}{R\rho T}u_{n}^{i}\mathbf{M}+\frac{T_{n}}{6\rho}\Big(\frac{|v-u|^{2}}{RT}-3\Big)\mathbf{M}\Big]+\mathbf{M}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\bf I-P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f_{n}].

From the coefficient equation
\varepsilon^{n-1}
with
1\leq n\leq 2k-1
in (
1.1.1
), it is straightforward to obtain the expression for microscopic part
f_{n}=\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}F_{n}
:

\displaystyle({\bf I-P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f_{n}]=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\mathbf{M}}^{-1}\Big[-\mathbf{M}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\partial_{t}F_{n-1}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}F_{n-1}

(4.5)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j=n-1\\
i,j\geq 0\end{subarray}}\Big(E_{n}+v\times B_{n}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{j}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j=n\\
i,j\geq 1\end{subarray}}\mathcal{C}(F_{i},F_{j})\Big)\Big],

and equations of
E_{n},B_{n}
:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}E_{n}-\nabla_{x}\times B_{n}=4\pi\big(u_{n}+\rho_{n}u\big),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}B_{n}+\nabla_{x}\times E_{n}=0,

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}\cdot E_{n}=-\rho_{n},\ \nabla_{x}\cdot B_{n}=0.

To obtain equations of
[\rho_{n},u_{n},T_{n}]
which corresponds to the macroscopic part of
f_{n}
, we need to project the equation of
f_{n}
in (
1.1.1
) onto
1,v,|v|^{2}
.
Before doing this, we first note that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}F_{n}dv=\rho_{n},\qquad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}vF_{n}dv=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}uF_{n}dv+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v-u)F_{n}dv=\rho_{n}u+u_{n},

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v^{i}v^{j}F_{n}dv=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[(v^{i}-u^{i})(v^{j}-u^{j})-\delta_{ij}\frac{|v-u|^{2}}{3}\Big]F_{n}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt+u_{n}^{i}u^{j}+\rho_{n}u^{i}u^{j}+u^{i}u_{n}^{j}+\frac{\delta_{ij}(3RT\rho_{n}+RTT_{n})}{3},

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v|^{2}F_{n}dv=\rho_{n}|u|^{2}+2u\cdot u_{n}+3RTT_{n}+RTT_{n},

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v^{i}|v|^{2}F_{n}dv=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v^{i}-u^{i})|v|^{2}F_{n}dv+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u^{i}|v|^{2}F_{n}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 65.44142pt=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v^{i}-u^{i})\big[|v-u|^{2}-5RT\big]F_{n}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt+2u^{j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[(v^{i}-u^{i})(v^{j}-u^{j})-\delta_{ij}\frac{|v-u|^{2}}{3}\Big]F_{n}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt+(5RT+|u|^{2})u_{n}^{i}+u^{i}\Big[(5RT+|u|^{2})\rho_{n}+2u\cdot u_{n}+\frac{5}{3}RTT_{n}\Big],

and

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v^{i}(E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{n}dv

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big[E^{i}+(v\times B)^{i}\big]F_{n}dv=\rho_{n}\big[E^{i}+\big(u\times B\big)^{i}\big]+\big(u_{n}\times B\big)^{i},

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v|^{2}(E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{n}dv=2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot EF_{n}dv=2E\cdot(\rho_{n}u+u_{n}).

Now we project the equation of
f_{n}
in (
1.1.1
) onto
1,v,|v|^{2}
to get

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\rho_{n}+\nabla_{x}\cdot(\rho_{n}u+u_{n})=0,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\rho_{n}u^{i}+u_{n}^{i})+\partial_{x_{j}}\Big[u_{n}^{i}u^{j}+\rho_{n}u^{i}u^{j}+u^{i}u_{n}^{j}+\frac{\delta_{ij}(3RT\rho_{n}+RTT_{n})}{3}\Big]

\displaystyle+\rho\big[E^{i}_{n}+\big(u\times B_{n}\big)^{i}\big]+\rho_{n}\big[E^{i}+\big(u\times B\big)^{i}\big]+\big(u_{n}\times B\big)^{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{x_{j}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[(v^{i}-u^{i})(v^{j}-u^{j})-\delta_{ij}\frac{|v-u|^{2}}{3}\Big]F_{n}dv

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{0}+j_{0}=n\\
i_{0},j_{0}\geq 1\end{subarray}}\Big[\rho_{j_{0}}\big[E^{i}_{i_{0}}+\big(u\times B_{i_{0}}\big)^{i}\big]+\big(u_{j_{0}}\times B_{i_{0}}\big)^{i}\Big],

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\big[\rho_{n}|u|^{2}+2u\cdot u_{n}+3RT\rho_{n}+RTT_{n}\big]

\displaystyle+\nabla_{x}\cdot\Big[(5RT+|u|^{2})(u_{n}+\rho_{n}u)+2u\cdot u_{n}+\frac{5}{3}RTT_{n}u\big)\Big]

\displaystyle+2\rho u\cdot E_{n}+2E\cdot(\rho_{n}u+u_{n})+2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{0}+j_{0}=n\\
i_{0},j_{0}\geq 1\end{subarray}}E_{i_{0}}\cdot(\rho_{j_{0}}u+u_{j_{0}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v-u)\big[|v-u|^{2}-5RT\big]F_{n}dv

\displaystyle-2\partial_{x_{j}}\Big[u^{i}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[(v^{i}-u^{i})(v^{j}-u^{j})-\delta_{ij}\frac{|v-u|^{2}}{3}\Big]F_{n}dv\Big].

By utilizing the compressible Euler-Maxwell system (
1.1.2
), we can express the aforementioned equations in a more concise form

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\rho_{n}+\nabla_{x}\cdot(\rho_{n}u+u_{n})=0,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u_{n}+(u\cdot\nabla_{x})u_{n}+(u_{n}\cdot\nabla_{x})u+(\nabla_{x}\cdot u)u_{n}+\frac{1}{3}\nabla_{x}(3RT\rho_{n}+RTT_{n})-\frac{R\rho_{n}}{\rho}\nabla_{x}\big(\rho T\big)

\displaystyle+\rho\big[E_{n}+\big(u\times B_{n}\big)\big]+u_{n}\times B+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j=n\\
i,j\geq 1\end{subarray}}\Big[\rho_{j}\big[E_{i}+\big(u\times B_{i}\big)\big]+\big(u_{j}\times B_{i}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[(v-u)\otimes(v-u)-\frac{|v-u|^{2}}{3}\mathbf{I}_{3\times 3}\Big]F_{n}dv,

\displaystyle RT\big[\partial_{t}T_{n}+u\cdot\nabla_{x}T_{n}+(\nabla_{x}\cdot u)T_{n}\big]+2RT\nabla_{x}\cdot u_{n}+3R\nabla_{x}T\cdot u_{n}

\displaystyle-\frac{2RT}{\rho}\nabla_{x}\rho\cdot u_{n}-2(u\times B)\cdot u_{n}

(4.6)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2u\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j=n\\
i,j\geq 1\end{subarray}}\big[\rho_{j}E_{n}+u_{j}\times B_{i}\big]-\nabla_{x}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v-u)\big[|v-u|^{2}-5RT\big]F_{n}dv

\displaystyle+2u^{i}\partial_{x_{j}}\Big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[(v^{i}-u^{i})(v^{j}-u^{j})-\delta_{ij}\frac{|v-u|^{2}}{3}\Big]F_{n}dv\Big],

\displaystyle-2\partial_{x_{j}}\Big[u^{i}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[(v^{i}-u^{i})(v^{j}-u^{j})-\delta_{ij}\frac{|v-u|^{2}}{3}\Big]F_{n}dv\Big],

\displaystyle\partial_{t}E_{n}-\nabla_{x}\times B_{n}=u_{n}+\rho_{n}u,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}B_{n}+\nabla_{x}\times E_{n}=0,

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}\cdot E_{n}=-\rho_{n},\ \nabla_{x}\cdot B_{n}=0,

where
1\leq n\leq 2k-1
and
\mathbf{I}_{3\times 3}
stands for the unit
3\times 3
matrix.

Following the approach in
[
33
]
and
[
37
]
, we can express (
4.2
) as a symmetric hyperbolic system. Consequently, we can construct classical solution
[\rho_{n},u_{n},T_{n},E_{n},B_{n}]
in the Sobolev space
H^{4k+4-2n}
with
1\leq n\leq 2k-1
inductively.

Step 2. Estimates of the coefficients.
We aim to establish (
4.3
) and (
4.4
), indicating the velocity decay estimates of
F_{n}
and the temporal growth of
F_{n},E_{n}
, and
B_{n}
with
1\leq n\leq 2k-1
, around the global Maxwellian
\mu
.

Letting
F_{n}=\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}h_{n}
for
1\leq n\leq 2k-1
, we decompose
h_{n}
as the macroscopic part and microscopic part:

\displaystyle h_{n}={\bf P}[h_{n}]+({\bf I-P})[h_{n}].

Next, from (
1.1.1
), one sees that
h_{n}
satisfies the following iterative equations

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}[h_{1}]+\mathcal{L}_{d}[h_{1}]=-\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\{\partial_{t}\mathbf{M}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbf{M}\}+\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{M},

\displaystyle\partial_{t}h_{1}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}h_{1}-(E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}h_{1}+\frac{v}{2RT_{c}}\cdot Eh_{1}

\displaystyle-h_{1}\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(E_{1}+v\times B_{1})\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{M}+\mathcal{L}[h_{2}]+\mathcal{L}_{d}[h_{2}]=\Gamma(h_{1},h_{1}),

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}h_{n}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}h_{n}-(E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}h_{n}+\frac{v}{2RT_{c}}\cdot Eh_{n}

\displaystyle-\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(E_{n}+v\times B_{n})\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{M}-{\bf 1}_{n>1}\sum\limits_{i+j=n\atop{i,j\geq 1}}(E_{i}+v\times B_{i})\cdot\nabla_{v}h_{j}

\displaystyle-{\bf 1}_{n>1}\sum\limits_{i+j=n\atop{i,j\geq 1}}\frac{v}{2RT_{c}}\cdot E_{i}h_{j}+\mathcal{L}[h_{n+1}]+\mathcal{L}_{d}[h_{n+1}]=\sum\limits_{i+j=n+1}\Gamma(h_{i},h_{j}),

\displaystyle\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots\cdots,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}h_{2k-2}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}h_{2k-2}-(E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}h_{2k-2}+\frac{v}{2RT_{c}}\cdot Eh_{2k-2}

\displaystyle-\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(E_{2k-2}+v\times B_{2k-2})\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{M}-\sum\limits_{i+j={2k-2}\atop{i,j\geq 1}}(E_{i}+v\times B_{i})\cdot\nabla_{v}h_{j}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{i+j={2k-2}\atop{i,j\geq 1}}\frac{v}{2RT_{c}}\cdot E_{i}h_{j}+\mathcal{L}[h_{2k-1}]+\mathcal{L}_{d}[h_{2k-1}]=\sum\limits_{i+j=2k-1\atop{i,j\geq 1}}\Gamma(h_{i},h_{j}),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}h_{2k-1}

\displaystyle+v\cdot\nabla_{x}h_{2k-1}-(E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}h_{2k-1}+\frac{v}{2RT_{c}}\cdot Eh_{2k-1}

\displaystyle-\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(E_{2k-1}+v\times B_{2k-1})\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{M}-\sum\limits_{i+j={2k-1}\atop{i,j\geq 1}}(E_{i}+v\times B_{i})\cdot\nabla_{v}h_{j}

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{i+j={2k-1}\atop{i,j\geq 1}}\frac{v}{2RT_{c}}\cdot E_{i}h_{j}=\sum\limits_{i+j=2k}\Gamma(h_{i},h_{j}).

To prove (
4.3
) and (
4.4
), our current objective is to demonstrate that

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N+4k-2n+2}

\displaystyle\left|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-n(1+|\gamma+2s|}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h_{n}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq N+2k-2n+2}\left|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[E_{n},B_{n}]\right|\leq C(C_{0})(1+t)^{n}

(4.7)

holds for
N\geq 4
.

In fact, (
4.7
) can be deduced from the following estimates:

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N+4k-2n+2}

\displaystyle\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-n-(2n-1)|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h_{n}\right\|_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq N+2k-2n+2}\left|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[E_{n},B_{n}]\right|\leq C(C_{0})(1+t)^{n}.

(4.8)

Then, using standard energy estimates, we can derive from (
4.2
) and (
1.13
) that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq m}

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[\rho_{n},u_{n},T_{n},E_{n},B_{n}]\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon_{1}(1+t)^{-p_{0}}\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[\rho_{n},u_{n},T_{n},E_{n},B_{n}]\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq m+1}\|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I-P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f_{n}]\|_{\bf D}\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq m}\|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[\rho_{n},u_{n},T_{n},E_{n},B_{n}]\|,

(4.9)

where
m>0
is finite.

Remark 4.1
.

It’s important to note that we use
({\bf I-P}_{\mathbf{M}})[f_{n}]
instead of
({\bf I-P}_{\mathbf{M}})[h_{n}]
to establish the above bound. This choice arises from an additional spatial derivative loss when dealing with
({\bf I-P}_{\mathbf{M}})[h_{n}]
. As per (
4.5
), this additional spatial derivative loss can be controlled by higher-order norms of the previous
n-1
items, namely
[\rho_{i},u_{i},T_{i},E_{i},B_{i}]
for
0\leq i\leq n-1
, through an inductive argument. However, this approach is not applicable for
h_{n}
because the equation for
h_{n}
involves an additional term
\mathcal{L}_{d}[({\bf I-P})[h_{n}]]
, which contains
[\rho_{n},u_{n},T_{n}]
. Consequently, it leads to another loss of one more spatial derivative for
[\rho_{n},u_{n},T_{n}]
, resulting in a situation where the estimates enter an endless loop.

To close our estimate, we now turn to estimate the microscopic part
({\bf I-P})[h_{n}]
.
For
n=1
, in view of the first equation for
\varepsilon^{0}
in (
1.1.1
), one has

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}[({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]],\langle v\rangle^{2(\ell-1)-|\gamma+2s|}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}_{d}[({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]],\langle v\rangle^{2(\ell-1)-|\gamma+2s|}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\left\langle\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\left[\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\{\partial_{t}\mathbf{M}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbf{M}\}\right],\langle v\rangle^{2(\ell-1)-|\gamma+2s|}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\rangle

(4.10)

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\left[\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{M}\right],\langle v\rangle^{2(\ell-1)-|\gamma+2s|}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\rangle.

For the 1st term in the right hand side of (
4.2
), by utilizing (
3.12
), and using the equivalence of norms for
{\bf P}[h_{1}]
and

{\bf P}[f_{1}]
, along with the following fact:

\|[\rho_{1},u_{1},T_{1}]\|^{2}_{H^{\alpha_{0}}_{t}H^{|\alpha|}_{x}}\lesssim\|{\bf P}[f_{1}]\|^{2}_{H^{\alpha_{0}}_{t}H^{|\alpha|}_{x}H^{|\beta|}_{v}}\lesssim\|[\rho_{1},u_{1},T_{1}]\|^{2}_{H^{\alpha_{0}}_{t}H^{|\alpha|}_{x}},

one has

\displaystyle\Big|

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\mathcal{L}_{d}[({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]],\langle v\rangle^{2(\ell-1)-|\gamma+2s|}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon_{1}\Big(\sum\limits_{\bar{\alpha}_{0}\leq\alpha_{0}}\sum_{\bar{\alpha}\leq\alpha,|\bar{\beta}|\leq|\beta|}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\bar{\alpha}_{0}}\partial^{\bar{\alpha}}_{\bar{\beta}}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}+\|[\rho_{1},u_{1},T_{1}]\|^{2}_{H^{\alpha_{0}}_{t}H^{|\alpha|}_{x}}\Big).

Additionally, considering (
1.13
), we can bound the 2nd and 3rd terms on the R.H.S. of (
4.2
) as follows:

\displaystyle\Big|\left\langle\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\left[\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\{\partial_{t}\mathbf{M}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbf{M}\}\right],\langle v\rangle^{2(\ell-1)-|\gamma+2s|}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\left\langle\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\left[\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}(E+v\times B)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{M}\right],\langle v\rangle^{2(\ell-1)}e^{2q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\epsilon_{1}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|_{\bf D}.

Consequently, we use (
(ii)
) to get for
\beta>0
that

\displaystyle\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\eta+C\epsilon_{1}\right)\sum_{\bar{\alpha}_{0}+|\bar{\alpha}|\leq\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|}\sum_{|\bar{\beta}|=|\beta|}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\bar{\alpha}_{0}}\partial^{\bar{\alpha}}_{\bar{\beta}}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}

\displaystyle-C(\eta)\sum\limits_{\bar{\alpha}_{0}\leq\alpha_{0}}\sum_{\bar{\alpha}\leq\alpha}\sum_{|\bar{\beta}|<|\beta|}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\bar{\alpha}_{0}}\partial^{\bar{\alpha}}_{\bar{\beta}}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|^{2}_{\bf D}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\|[\rho_{1},u_{1},T_{1}]\|^{2}_{H^{\alpha_{0}}_{t}H^{|\alpha|}_{x}}\Big),

and for
\beta=0

\displaystyle\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}

\displaystyle-C\epsilon_{1}{\bf 1}_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|>0}\sum\limits_{\bar{\alpha}_{0}\leq\alpha_{0},\bar{\alpha}\leq\alpha,|\bar{\alpha}_{0}|+|\bar{\alpha}|<|\alpha_{0}|+|\alpha|}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\bar{\alpha}_{0}}\partial^{\bar{\alpha}}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}

\displaystyle-C\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times B_{C}(\eta))}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big(\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\|[\rho_{1},u_{1},T_{1}]\|^{2}_{H^{\alpha_{0}}_{t}H^{|\alpha|}_{x}}\Big),

and moreover

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}\leq C\Big(\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\|[\rho_{1},u_{1},T_{1}]\|^{2}_{H^{\alpha_{0}}_{t}H^{|\alpha|}_{x}}\Big).

To summarize the above estimates, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq m+4k-4}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{1}]\right\|_{\bf D}^{2}\leq C\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(4.11)

Thus (
4.11
) and (
4.2
) with
n=1
gives

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq m+4k-4}

\displaystyle\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-1-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h_{1}(t)\right\|_{\bf D}+\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq m+4k-4}\left|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[E_{1},B_{1}]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq m+4k-4}\left\|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[\rho_{1},u_{1},T_{1},E_{1},B_{1}](0,x)\right\|+C\epsilon_{1}(1+t).

(4.12)

Now we assume that

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq m+4k-2n-2}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-n-(2n-1)|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h_{n}(t)\right\|_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq m+4k-2n-2}\left|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[E_{n},B_{n}]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq m+4k-2n-2}\left\|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[\rho_{n},u_{n},T_{n},E_{n},B_{n}](0,x)\right\|+C(C_{0})(1+t)^{n}

holds for
1\leq n\leq 2k-2
, then by repeating the argument used to deduce (
4.11
), one has

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq m+4k-2n-3}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-n-|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}({\bf I-P})[h_{n+1}](t)\right\|_{\bf D}\leq C(C_{0})(1+t)^{n}.

(4.13)

On the other hand, as discussed in Remark
4.1
, by a similar argument as for deriving (
4.13
), it can be demonstrated that
({\bf I-P})[f_{n}]
is bounded in the way as
({\bf I-P})[h_{n}]
.

Thus (
4.13
) together with (
4.2
) yields

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq m+4k-2n-4}

\displaystyle\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell-n-(2n-1)|\gamma+2s|/2}e^{q\langle v\rangle}\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h_{n+1}(t)\right\|_{\bf D}

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{\alpha_{0}+|\alpha|\leq m+4k-2n-4}\left|\partial_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\partial^{\alpha}[E_{n+1},B_{n+1}]\right|

(4.14)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(C_{0})(1+t)^{n+1}.

Finally, by setting
m=N+4
, the estimate (
4.2
) for
0\leq n\leq 2k-2
implies (
4.8
).

∎
